(33b)

for
0\geq p\geq N
. As we explore in the following examples, these simplified transport equations are common when describing the evolution of different fields.

Figure 1:
Flow diagram depicting the sequence of conditions (green diamonds) satisfied by the original universe of equations of motion (orange parallelograms) leading to relevant results (yellow rectangles) and corresponding examples (blue parallelograms with round vertices). The latter are presented in Secs.
IV
and
V
. This diagram does not exhaust all possibilities of conditional tests, since F-diagonality, C-diagonality and G-diagonality could, in principle, be tested independently, rather than displayed in serialized form. As such, this flowchart is just a visual representation of interesting combinations among the three diagonality conditions that lead to the relevant universal results presented in Sec.
III
.

IV
Examples in Lorentzian spacetimes

To illustrate the extent of the results we have just derived, let us now consider several examples of fields whose dynamics is described by a wave-like equation in the form of Eq. (
3a
). We start by considering several fields on a prescribed Lorentzian spacetime which evolve in vacuum (only under the influence of gravity). Also, by noting that the leading-order and first subleading-order results (
p=n,n+1
) have equal powers of the wave vectors
\underset{I}{q}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}
appearing in both sides of every equation, we can substitute
\underset{I}{q}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}
by
\underset{I}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}
, and recast our derivations in terms of the actual physical wave vector
\underset{I}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}
.

IV.1
No coupling, FCG-diagonal kinetic tensors and vanishing friction tensors

First, we consider noncoupled fields in Lorentzian spacetimes, with examples being the Klein-Gordon scalar
\Phi
, the electromagnetic potential vector
A_{\mu}
, the Faraday tensor
F_{\mu\nu}
, and a trace-reversed gravitational wave (GW) perturbation in general relativity
C_{\mu\nu}
. A thorough study of these fields can be found in various classical texts
[
14
,
1
,
18
]
as well as in more recent works
[
19
,
28
]
. Their second-order equations of motion read, respectively:

\widehat{\square}\,\Phi-\left(\xi\widehat{R}+\mu^{2}\right)\Phi=0\,,

(34)

\widehat{\square}\,A_{\mu}-\mathchoice{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,A_{\nu}=0\,,\quad\left(\widehat{\nabla}_{\alpha}A^{\alpha}=0\right)\,,

(35)

\widehat{\square}\,F_{\mu\nu}+\mathchoice{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,F_{\lambda\sigma}+2\mathchoice{\widehat{R}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.12622pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.12622pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.3136pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.08113pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}_{{{[\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2 (36)

and

(37)

We have included auxiliary gauge conditions inside parentheses to remind ourselves that they must be further enforced to constrain the general solutions of the second-order equations of motion. Particularly, in the case of
F_{\mu\nu}
, which we describe by its second-order wave equation in Lorentzian spacetimes, first-order Maxwell equations act as gauge conditions of sorts, having to be later imposed in order to remove nonphysical solutions. In the equations above,
\,\widehat{\cdot}\,
represents
\,\cdot\,
computed using the Levi-Civita connection (since we are in the context of Lorentzian spacetimes), whereas
\,\underline{\hbox{$\cdot$}}\,
denotes
\,\cdot\,
computed on a background gravitational field, which in this case acts as the prescribed metric [cf. the comment after Eq. (
3b
)].

A quick inspection shows that all of these fields obey equations of motion possessing FCG-diagonal kinetic tensors, vanishing friction tensors, and mass tensors differing according to the field rank. Table
1
summarizes them in a compact form:

index for different fields has been dropped due to the absence of coupling.

From this observation, by proposing JWKB Ansätze:

\underset{}{\Psi}\mathchoice{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}(x,\epsilon)=\left[\,\sum_{p=0}^{N}\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p)}(x)\,\left(\frac{\epsilon}{i}\right)^{p}\,\right]\,e^{iS(x)/\epsilon}\,,

(38)

and following the general procedure we have developed in the prior sections (notice that we have dropped the
I
index due to the absence of coupling), the wave vectors
k_{\mu}
associated to each Ansatz satisfy [cf. Eq. (
23
)]:

k_{\mu}\,k^{\mu}=0\Rightarrow k^{\nu}\widehat{\nabla}_{\nu}k^{\mu}=0\,,

(39)

i.e.
, their associated integral curves are
null
and both
affinely parametrized metric geodesics and autoparallels
[since the RHS of Eq. (
24
) vanishes for Lorentzian spacetimes]. Moreover, provided friction tensors vanish altogether, their leading-order amplitudes satisfy a simplified version of Eq. (
33a
):

\underset{}{\widehat{\mathscr{D}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}=\left(2\,\underset{}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\widehat{\nabla}_{\alpha}+\widehat{\nabla}_{\alpha}\underset{}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\right)\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}=0\,,

(40)

where
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}
is equal to
\underset{}{\phi}_{(0)}
,
\underset{}{a}_{(0)\mu}
,
\underset{}{f}_{(0)\mu\nu}
, and
\underset{}{c}_{(0)\mu\nu}
, respectively,
i.e.
, the leading-order amplitudes of the JWKB Ansätze for the Klein-Gordon scalar, the electromagnetic vector potential, the Faraday tensor and the GW perturbation, respectively. Next, with the exception of the Klein-Gordon field (which is a scalar), the gauge conditions appearing inside parentheses for
A_{\mu}
,
F_{\mu\nu}
and
C_{\mu\nu}
imply that
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}
are transverse to their corresponding wave vectors
k^{\mu}
. Therefore, since GO results do not depend on mass tensors, by knowing that all of the above fields obey equations of motion with FCG-diagonal kinetic tensors and vanishing friction tensors, they should all obey the following
laws of geometrical optics
:

(i)

the associated wave vector
k^{\mu}
is null and (affine and metric) geodesic,

(ii)

the leading-order amplitude
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}
is transverse to
k^{\mu}
(with the exception of the Klein-Gordon scalar), and

(iii)

\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}
evolves along the integral curves of
k^{\mu}
according to Eq. (
40
).

Since Eq.
40
is a tensor equation, when we are describing light propagation in the geometrical optics limit, either using
A_{\mu}
or
F_{\mu\nu}
as the fundamental field, law (iii) can be recast as two indepedent transport laws for the intensity and the polarization of light. The former equation gives the pleasant result that a notion of “photon number” is preserved along the selected null geodesic
[
1
,
18
,
28
]
. In turn, the latter transport law simplifies to a parallel transport provided the chosen set of instantaneous observers along the selected geodesic is also parallel along the null curve or possesses special kinematic quantities which further simplify the general transport law
[
28
]
. In the case of a GW in GR, provided a null tetrad decomposition for
\underset{}{c}_{(0)\mu\nu}
is performed, law (iii) also expresses that a notion of “polarization” for the GW is parallel transported along
k^{\mu}

[
19
]
.

Finally, subleading-order amplitudes
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p)}
(
1\leq p\leq N
) satisfy a simplified form of Eq. (
33b
), but, contrary to the one followed by
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}
, including a nonhomogenous term derived from the immediately superleading-order amplitude:

\underset{}{\mathscr{D}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p+1)}=-\underset{}{\mathcal{L}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}\,\underset{}{\psi}{}^{B}_{(p)}\,,\quad 0\leq p\leq N\,,

(41)

where
\underset{}{\mathcal{L}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}
is determined from the kinetic and mass tensors appearing in Table
1
for each tensor field. Eq. (
41
) gives the
beyond geometrical optics
transport equations for
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p)}
along the integral curves of the corresponding wave vectors, comprising a set of perturbative equations in which the source term of the transport equation for
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p+1)}
only depends on the solution of the transport equation for the immediately superleading-order amplitude
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(p)}
. These additional transport laws allow one to obtain higher-order corrections to the amplitude associated to the Ansatz of a given field, thus refining the full JWKB amplitude beyond its leading-order component
\underset{}{\psi}{}^{A}_{(0)}

[
19
,
22
]
.

IV.2
Coupling and FC-diagonal kinetic tensors: reduced Horndeski theories

After illustraing the generality of our results by considering four examples with FCG-diagonal kinetic tensors and vanishing friction tensors in the previous section, let us turn our attention to a final example in Lorentzian spacetimes, namely, scalar-tensor GWs in a subclass of Horndeski theories of gravity
[
33
,
34
]
referred to as
reduced Horndeski theories
(RHTs)
[
24
,
25
,
26
,
27
]
, whose constraints guarantee that scalar-tensor GWs propagate at the speed of light.

Similar to the procedure for GWs in GR, second-order equations of motion can be derived for a GW perturbation pair
(\Phi,H_{\mu\nu})
by linearizing the corresponding field equations in RHTs. These are coupled equations at zeroth, first and second order derivatives, but as shown in Ref.
[
25
]
, one can perform a diagonalization procedure to decouple the kinetic terms (second-order derivatives) in the equations for
\Phi
and
H_{\mu\nu}
, arriving at a new pair (
\underset{1}{\Psi}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}=\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}\hss}\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}}\Phi,\underset{2}{\Psi}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}=\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}\hss}\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}
) subject to the following equations of motion:

\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\underset{11}{\mathcal{L}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}&\underset{12}{\mathcal{L}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}} (43)

with the kinetic, friction, and mass terms determined from the RHT Lagrangian and evaluated in the prescribed background scalar-tensor field
(\underaccent{\bar}{\varphi},\underaccent{\bar}{g}_{\mu\nu})
[cf. the comment after Eq. (

as the kernel letter for the bilinear form without risk of confusion with a general affine connection
\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}
, since parallel transport in Lorentzian spacetimes is given by the Levi-Civita connection of a given metric, which we denote by
\left\{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}\!{}^{{{\alpha}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\!{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu}}}\right\}
[cf. Eq. (
75
)]. In recent literature
[
25
]
, this new pair is interpreted as the true and independent degrees of freedom of scalar-tensor GWs in RHTs, obtained from a transformation that makes
\underset{12}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebo . The JWKB Ansätze in this case read

\underset{}{\Phi}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}(x,\epsilon)=\left[\,\sum_{p=0}^{N}\underset{}{\phi}_{(p)}(x)\,\left(\frac{\epsilon}{i}\right)^{p}\,\right]\,e^{iS_{1}(x)/\epsilon}\,,\quad N\geq 0\,,

(44)

and

\underset{}{\Gamma}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}(x,\epsilon)=\left[\,\sum_{p=0}^{N}\underset{}{\gamma}_{(p)\mu\nu}(x)\,\left(\frac{\epsilon}{i}\right)^{p}\,\right]\,e^{iS_{2}(x)/\epsilon}\,,\quad N\geq 0\,.

(45)

Now, contrary to the other examples in Lorentzian spacetimes, the kinetic tensors in RHTs are not necessarily FCG-diagonal. However, one can arrive at an FCG-diagonal kinetic tensor for the bilinear form
\Gamma_{\mu\nu}
by selecting the so-called and always achievable
harmonic gauge

[
24
,
25
]

\underaccent{\bar}{\hatnabla}_{\nu}\Gamma^{\mu\nu}=0\,,

(46)

which yields

\underset{22}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda to0.0pt{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}\hss}\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}}=-\frac{1}{2}G_{4}(\underaccent{\bar}{\varphi})=\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}\hss}\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}}-\frac{1}{2}\underaccent{\bar}{G}_{4}\,,

(47)

where
\underaccent{\bar}{G}_{4}
is one of the nonvanishing terms in the general Lagrangian of RHTs
[
24
]
. Since
\underaccent{\bar}{G}_{4}\neq 0
, the form of
\underset{22}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu\nu}}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.37701pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.65826pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.04161pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{{\lambda implies that the wave vector associated to

satisfies [cf. Eq. (

\underaccent{\bar}{g}^{\alpha\beta}\,\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77 (48)

textiti.e., the integral curves of
\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
are
null
and
affinely parametrized affine and metric geodesics
. On the other hand, the harmonic gauge does not constrain
\underset{11}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
in any way. However, since

is a scalar field,
i.e.
, it only possesses a single degree of freedom,
\underset{11}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
is trivially an FC-diagonal kinetic tensor, meaning that Eq. (

\underset{1}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}

\underset{\underaccent{\bar}{2}}{\widehat{\mathscr{D}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(g)}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.40248pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.32404pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51718pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\,\gamma_{(0)\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{\underset{2}{\overset{(1)}{\varkappa}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\tex (51)

where
\underset{\underaccent{\bar}{1}}{\widehat{\mathscr{D}}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{(\mathcal{K})}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.62221pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.44446pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(\mathcal{K})}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.62221pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.44446pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(\mathcal{K})}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.62221pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.44446pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{(\mathcal{K})}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.36813pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.62221pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.44446pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\mathrel{\hbox to0.0pt{\raisebox{1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}\hss}\raisebox{-1.07639pt}{$\cdot$}}=\underset{11}{\mathcal{K}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}\,\underset{\underaccent{\bar}{1}}{\widehat{D}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}}{{}^{{{}\ma . At last, the leading-order relation derived from the harmonic gauge condition for
\Gamma_{\mu\nu}
implies that
\gamma_{(0)\mu\nu}
is transverse to
\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
. The above results can be summarized as the
laws of geometrical optics
for scalar-tensor GWs in RHTs:

(i)

the scalar pertubation wave vector
\underset{1}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
is not null nor (affine and metric) geodesic in general. If, however,
\underset{1}{\overset{(2)}{\varkappa}}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}=0
,
\underset{1}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
is null and (affine and metric) geodesic, a situation that significantly simplifies the study of scalar-tensor GWs in RHTs

]
.

(ii)

the tensor perturbation wave vector
\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
is always null and (affine and metric) geodesic.

(iii)

the leading-order amplitude
\underset{}{\phi}_{(0)}
of the scalar perturbation has no transversality condition with respect to
\underset{1}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
given its scalar character.

(iv)

the leading-order amplitude
\underset{}{\gamma}_{(0)\mu\nu}
of the tensor perturbation is transverse to
\underset{2}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
.

(v)

\underset{}{\phi}_{(0)}
evolves according to Eq. (
50
).

(vi)

\underset{}{\gamma}_{(0)\mu\nu}
evolves according Eq. (
51
).

\gamma_{(p)\lambda\sigma}\,e^{iS_{2}/\epsilon}\end{array}\right]\,,\end{split}

(53)

By analogy with the previous examples, Eqs. (
52
) and (
53
) give, respectively, the
beyond geometrical optics
transport equations for
\underset{}{\phi}_{(p)}
and
\underset{}{\gamma}_{(p)\mu\nu}
(
1\leq p\leq N
) along the integral curves of the corresponding wave vectors. In spite of the fact that
\underset{1}{k}\mathchoice{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}{{}^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.0pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{}}}}
is not null in general, its associated dispersion operator vanishes [cf. (
20a
)]. As such, all transport equation in this example also comprise a set of perturbative equations, with the source term on the RHS of the transport equations for
\underset{}{\phi}_{(p+1)}
and
\underset{}{\gamma}_{(p+1)\mu\nu}
only depending on the solutions of the transport equations for the immediately superleading-order amplitudes
\underset{}{\phi}_{(p)}
and
\underset{}{\gamma}_{(p)\mu\nu}
(
0\leq p\leq N-1
). Notice however, that coupling is generally still present. These additional transport laws allow one to obtain higher-order corrections to the amplitudes associated to the Ansätze of the two fields, thus refining the full JWKB amplitudes beyond their leading-order components
\underset{I}{\psi}{}^{A_{I}}_{(0)}
.

V
Examples in metric-affine spacetimes

In the previous section, we covered five different examples in Lorentzian spacetimes which show how the universal results of Sec.
III
can be directly applied to obtain the laws of geometrical optics for a number of tensor fields. Now, the results of Sec.
III
were in fact derived for metric-affine spacetimes, which naturally include Lorentzian spacetimes but also modified theories of gravity that include torsion and/or nonmetricity to describe additional degrees of freedom of the gravitational field [cf. App. (
A
)]. We now go over two examples in this more general geometrical context in the case of the Faraday tensor.

V.1
Faraday tensor in spacetimes with a metric-compatible connection and a completely antisymmetric torsion

In a spacetime with a metric-compatible connection (
\mathchoice{Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}=0
) and a completely antisymmetric torsion (
\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu\alpha}}}}=-\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\mu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\mu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\mu\alpha}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\mu\alpha}}}}=\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.0768pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu\alpha\mu}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.07036pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.50754pt][c]{$\scrip
